      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The UHPIC unlocks ZPM Energy Hatches and Dynamos, as well as the Active Transformer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You may know the drill, but do you know the screwdriver?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ultra High Power Integrated Circuit",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Dielectric PCB Coolant is used to maintain some highly advanced late and end-game machinery.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You only need a little bit in LuV, but you'll want to automate it when you get to ZPM.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Absolutely beneficial for the environment",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Dielectric PCB Coolant",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Active Transformer is a multiblock used to transform energy on a much larger scale. For example, you can convert HV to LV power directly by using the apropriate energy hatches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "It also has a second function, detailed in the above quest.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This is yet another feature from TecTech, which also required these cables to be painted. They do look fantastic if you paint them, but it's optional in CEu.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "More than a large transformer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Active Transformer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Data Bank is a multiblock capable of storing huge amounts of data. You can fill it with Data Access Hatches, and fill those with Data Storage Items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "After that, you'll need power, and then a way to connect it to your Assembly Line! This is done through Optical Fiber Cables, which can be used to link Optical Data Hatches together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "An Optical Transmission Hatch is used to send data (like an output), and an Optical Reception Hatch to recieve the data (like an input).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Optical cables have some restrictions however. They can only connect one input to one output. The Data Bank is your answer to this, since you can place many hatches on them to route your data anywhere.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You can use this behavior to chain Data Banks as well. Connecting one Data Bank to another, and then one of them to an Assembly Line will provide the data of both banks!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: This multiblock will be required later on in progression, but is very useful to set up early.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This multiblock also originates from TecTech, but part of its mechanics required optical cables to be painted as well. You don't have to do that here, but it looks really nice if you do!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Time for a server room",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Mass Data Storage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Active Transformer has a second use - Laser Power. Lasers are like superconductors, but behave slightly differently. They share the energy input across all connected active transformers, allowing widespread power distribution. You can use Active Transformers to convert regular EU to and from Lasers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Like optical cables, they can only be connected to one input and output. But, these ones also only can move in straight lines. To turn them, an Active Transformer with a Laser Source Hatch (output) and Target Hatch (input) is needed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This is yet again another TecTech feature, but there's a bit more to it this time. TecTech's lasers were tiered by voltage and carried thousands of amps instead. CEu doesn't faff around with any of that tiering. You only need one type of laser here!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some lasers!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Laser Power Transmission",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Doping Silicon with Naquadah will give a better Silicon Boule.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Use it for higher yield on Wafers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This will also unlock the best recipe for HV Circuits in the LuV Circuit Assembler. You're probably going to want to mass produce these for the many Lapotronic Energy Orbs that'll be required moving forwards.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Doper than ever before",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah-doped Silicon Boule",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah is a late-game material with a variety of uses. It is required when building a Fusion Reactor and progressing to ZPM, to name a few.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For some reason, its fluid is a brilliant green - don't ask us why.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Naquadah is a material originally from Stargate.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't you mean Stargatium?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Coils crank up the Heat Capacity to 7,200K.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "These coils are required for progressing to the second half of LuV and beyond.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Well... not some \"juicy GregTech lore\", but a cool space fact, owing to the fact you're beating the Sun's temperature with your EBF. The surface temperature of the Sun is 5,800K. We're taking this as an effective temperature - i.e. the radiative temperature of the Sun as if it were a perfectly \"black body\". Check out the Stefan-Boltzmann law if you're interested in some crude astrophysics.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Oh, and the \"photosphere\" (the part of the sun we're talking about when we mention its surface)? It's 400 kilometres thick. That's 250 miles. Damn!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hotter than the surface temperature of the Sun!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Coils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Superconductors are necessary if you're eyeing a Fusion Reactor.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't ignore our warning here - you're gonna need a lot of them. Luckily, any Indium shortages will be solved by your Naquadah line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Be glad these don't use pumps",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Superconductors",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Plasmas are extremely hot, with temperatures exceeding 30k Kelvin!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "However, this doesn't mean that a special type of pipe is required to transport plasma. Plasmas are treated slightly differently, and bend the rules a bit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid pipes that can handle plasmas, such as Tungsten, Naquadah, or Duranium can hold any plasma regardless of temperature. This means they won't melt under the heat of the plasma!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "WARNING: Plasmas in incompatible pipes will cause voiding, pipe burning, and disproportionately high rates of death. Be careful!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: This mechanic originates from GT6, just like the acids!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The stars are calling...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid of the Stars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Use this turbine to extract very large amounts of power from plasma!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Helium Plasma is the best fuel for its cost.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "If you're the type to question how realistic Plasma Turbines are... look up Magnetohydrodynamic Generators if you want something that bends fact into fiction.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plasma? Turbine?! How?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Large Plasma Turbine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This is the main material for ZPM. Having this in stock will be a test of your Platinum processing, so this is a pre-emptive warning to stay on top of mining PGS-bearing ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Prepare to make lots... after you make Fusion and Crystal Circuits. Tackle those before you try making this.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Stargate's Strongest",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah Alloy",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/luv__ludicrous_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Refining Naquadah into further products will be your next automation challenge. The primary outputs of this line are Enriched Naquadah, Naquadria, and Trinium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "luv__ludicrous_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "ZPM voltage-tier components and machine parts."
    },

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "tips__transportation",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Sloop, traversing large sized bodies of water should be relatively easy. To make sure you dont end up at the mercy of the wind you can lower your Anchor to stop in place.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye your sails with dye of your choice!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Sloop.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Try not to sail through rivers with a sloop, it will likely NOT work as expected!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Where's the Wind Waker when you need it?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #alekiships:sloops",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Sloop",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Rowboat, you can now transport a decent amount of items across the oceans, perfect for overseas exploration, perhaps to the tropics! Make sure you create two Oars so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Rowboat.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tips: Two passenger slots? Perfect for bringing home pairs of breeding animals for your farm through a river system or across lakes and oceans.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Rowboat-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #alekiships:rowboats",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Rowboat",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Kayak in hand, you can now enjoy your quick solo journeys on water. Make sure you craft a Kayak Paddle (the canoe one won't work!) to go even faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Kayak-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Kayak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Canoe, traversing bodies of water in the early game will now be possible. Make sure you create a Canoe Paddle so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at the finished Canoe.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak is the second most simple boat you can craft, requiring Waxes for the Waterproof Hides. It is also the only boat that can be picked up as an item. Despite having a single passenger slot, it is the fastest boat in the game until the steel age Sloop!",

          "text": "The Kayak",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Requiring more complex materials, locking it to the bronze age, the Rowboat has capacity for 2 passengers and 2 storage, or 4 storage with no passengers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need to find some sort of Hardwood, detailed in the Field Guide, which also includes the construction process.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Keep in mind the boat must be constructed with the same wood! Do not mix different kinds of hardwoods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        },
        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Sloop is the most advanced seaworthy vessel, requiring Wrought Iron. It has ample space for storage, anvils and workbenches! It is incredibly expensive and requires practice to sail. The Field Guide has all the details for construction and on how to sail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",

        },

      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven is an electric equivalent to the Coke Oven. It can produce Coal Coke and Charcoal with a Creosote byproduct just as before, but also has additional Organic Byproducts, the most beneficial of these being Wood Tar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Another product is Biomass, which is more pertinent for your progression as of this moment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The byproducts can be Distilled for an array of organic resources, with different byproducts giving different ratios of different chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need to take a peek at the Distillation Quest to get to grips with the power of distillation...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are recipes that can accept Nitrogen to double the speed. This isn't quite worth pursuing right now, but Nitrogen will eventually be free and infinite.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You should be more than fine with a single LV Energy Hatch, and Superconductors to supply a steady 64 EU/t. Additionally, Cupronickel Coils are perfect when you're getting used to the Pyrolyse Oven, but you may want to upgrade to Kanthal eventually.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Plant path is one path you can take towards Ethylene, which is a necessary chemical in progressing to HV. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven remains optional. However, it is very rewarding to construct one either for Power, or for the various Byproducts, and we highly recommend you give it a look.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: In GT5u, the structure for the Pyrolyse Oven was infamously a giant box with only 9 coils inside. Players usually spent several minutes debugging what is wrong due to a lack of a EMI preview.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Thermal Decomposition",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Pyrolyse Oven",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to the organic Ethylene path! The goal here is to produce Ethanol and mix it with Sulfuric Acid to make Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To get Ethanol, you'll need to distill it from Biomass—so let's look at the different ways to produce that biomass.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The first method is simple: put saplings into a Brewery with water. The downside? It's slow.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The second method is more complex, but yields much better results. You’ll need to get Bio Chaff, which comes from macerating Plant Balls. Note that you won't get any extra chance until you unlock HV Crushing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plant Balls can be obtained by:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging Sugarcane or Beets",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging every wood that you can tap #afc:tappable_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Compressing saplings directly",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, process your Bio Chaff with water in a Brewery or go for a Pyrolyse Oven for a 66 percent boost in output.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You may recognise Plantballs from IndustrialCraft2! In IC2 Experimental, there was this rather... obscure way to turn Biomass into Biogas for power, which sadly required too much investment to be really worth it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plants aren't industrial enough!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery can be used to make Lubricant from Redstone and Creosote/Oil. Lubricant has some niche uses, namely being used in the Cutter to significantly reduce the duration of its recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery is a very, very slow machine, but it requires almost no power to run its recipes. Building many Breweries will help you obtain enough Biomass on your quest for plastic.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Brewery unlocks the Biomass path towards Ethylene. The same can be done in the Pyrolyse Oven. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You could make your own Brewery if you want to build up a company and start selling Booze... in GregTech 6.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "*hic*... *hic*",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_brewery)item(gtceu:mv_brewery))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Local Brewery selling Booze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Our warmest congratulations on making it here.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The way you should obtain Ethylene was described in the previous Quest, depending on the route you chose.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ethylene will shortly be used in polymer production. It's also useful in a few other reactions.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "One of many hydrocarbons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Put your previously made Biomass into a MV Distillery to make Ethanol.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this for Ethylene by reacting it with Sulfuric Acid in a MV Chemical Reactor... unless you chose to go down the Oil path.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you have a hefty starting stock of Ethanol, consider making and distilling Fermented Biomass in a Distillation Tower (when you get there!) for extra chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Do not try to use Ethanol as a Power source... it's quite possibly its worst use. If it's organic Power that you want, we suggest taking a look at Benzene instead!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Yo, wait - that's alcohol!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Distillating Oil or Raw Oil will give you Fuel that you will need to desulfurize.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hydrogen Sulfide is perfectly recycled in an Electrolyzer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To automate this process, simply place your Chemical Reactor and your Electrolyzer next to each other. Be sure to use your Screwdriver to enable input from the output side.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha is a good Product source, surprsingly it's also a really good fuel (we heard nerf is coming) but if you are sitting on Oil sand or you don't have Raw Oil then Light Oil may be better. Keep following the quests to the right for more details.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We're still confused on how Naphtha is pronounced",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha or Light Oil Residue",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't panic! We'll mostly be doing this to get hydrocarbons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are many ways to acquire Ethylene from Oil processing. It's easy to get lost in all the options and recipes!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The best way is to steam-crack Naphtha in a Chemical Reactor. Be sure to make the Severely Steam-Cracked version for a higher yield of Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once in HV, you'll have the option to do this recipe in the Cracking Unit at 100 percent efficiency. Unfortunately, the recipes you are doing with the Chemical Reactor have a loss of 50 percent.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: All of this looks and feels terribly inefficient - we hope that's strong motivation to tier up! The Distillation Tower will be a huge upgrade for petrochem, but you're not quite there... yet. Right now, you'll have to use a Distillery to get Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fuel and oil cracking can get complicated...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fuel Cracking",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hello there, and welcome to MV Energy Production.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "In this quest, we’ll go over the different options available to keep your MV machines powered and running smoothly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We won’t cover this in every chapter, so we highly recommend that you read the dedicated chapter on GregTech Energy for a deeper understanding of how everything works.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For now, here are your three main options:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use an MV Generator to directly produce MV-level energy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Step up your LV power by using transformers, with a 4:1 ratio between LV and MV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Convert RF using your Alternator to get MV energy directly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Higher-tier Generators will increase their fuel consumption proportionally to their Generation rate, keeping the duration of fuels and the specific amount of energy from fuels the same. The Advanced Steam Turbine will use 5,120 mB Steam per second, equivalent to 256 mB/t.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Get any of the generators to complete this quest, but you may prefer starting off with the Advanced Steam Turbine.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "HV voltage-tier components and machine parts."
    },
