      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "The Zero Point Module (item) is a non-rechargable battery with TONS of energy stored. You may have found one of these before now, but you haven't been able to discharge it until reaching ZPM.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "These can only be found as loot from Jungle Temples. They're exceptionally rare - don't worry about finding one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "This quest is optional and is just for fun. Feel free to hit the checkbox if you just want the completion points!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Zero Point Module, the full name of the ZPM tier, is also from Stargate. Its namesake is a device which generated crazy amounts of energy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Unlimited powah!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Zero Point Module",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "ZPM Superconductors are a major gate between you and a Fusion Reactor Mk2, which uses copious amounts for its structure blocks.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "You can also use them to make Superconducting Coils for cheaper.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Getting tired of all these pitch-black ingots yet?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "ZPM Superconductors",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "ZPM Components thankfully aren't too much harder than LuV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "They use lots of Osmiridium, so make sure your platinum refining process is up to the task.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Prepare your platline!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Absolute Elation",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Enriched Naquadah is an isotope of Naquadah, Nq-528. It is radioactive and will be used in (as of yet) unimplemented nuclear reactors.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Currently, you just need it to make Trinium Coils.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah, but radioactive!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Enriched Naquadah",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Trinium Coils bring up the Heat Capacity to 9,001K.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "These coils are required for progressing to the second half of ZPM, and beyond.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "IT'S OVER 9000!!!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Trinium Coils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "The Research Station is the upgrade to the Scanner. It's used for more advanced forms of research, and will finally allow you to perform research which requires Data Orbs and Modules.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "When running the Research Station, energy and Computation is needed. The latter is provided through an HPCA, which is explained in the next quest. It is recieved with a Computation Data Reception Hatch. Note that only one Reception Hatch is allowed in a Research Station.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "This multiblock needs computation sporadically, which means the recipe will not reverse progress if you don't supply enough - it will simply halt in-place. However, the machine will still draw energy constantly, even when recieving no computation.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "To scan items, the process is relatively similar. In the Object Holder, place the target item in the center slot, and the data storage item in the other slot. Then provide computation, and the data storage item will be updated upon completion. When running, the target item will be Locked and cannot be removed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Note: To form this multiblock, the Object Holder must face the controller.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Your research adventures continue!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Research Station",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "The UV Mainframe is required to move into Fusion Mk2.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "It's also made in the Assembly Line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Passing over the ingredients we've already seen before, watch out for the HSS-E Frames.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "...ah, here we go!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "First UV Circuits!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "The Crystal SoC is used to make cheaper Crystal Circuits. Enjoy the discount!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Crystal SoCs require ZPM Emitters, which means you need a Data Orb for their research.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "In the future, these may become more difficult to make.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: In GT5u, the texture for this item was red, and did not have the special design on it. There was a \"raw\" version with the design, but it was used for other unrelated things. Who knows why?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "When engraving it once isn't enough",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Crystal SoC",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "This hatch allows you to store more Data Storage Items than the basic one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Don't forget that this hatch requires a Data Orb for research.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Increased storage density!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Advanced Data Access Hatch",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadria is an isotope of Naquadah, Nq-522. It's highly unstable, but it'll still be used for UV components, Fusion Mk2, and when fusing Neutronium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Naquadah, but radioactive-er!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Production of Sterilized Growth Medium is the final processing line in GTCEu, for now.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this for your first Wetware Circuits.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Producing it won't be as much of a hassle as PBI, but nearly every step requires a Sterilized Cleanroom. This means you'll have to get creative with how you use your space.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Make sure to scrutinise every step of the chain. Some recipes are much more efficient than others!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Pro Tip: You can wall-share cleanrooms with doors in-between too if you want more space.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: GTCEu's Sterilized Growth Medium chain is loosely based off of Bartworks' from GTNH. The main difference is that GTNH required a Bacterial Vat multiblock, which was more complex than the ordered Assembly Line. GTCEu is considering adding a multiblock like it but with their own spin in the future.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "More Lore: In Gregicality Legacy, the Sterilized Growth Medium production chain was very different. It was one of the most difficult processes in the mod, with over 30 complicated steps! It was based off of producing different real-life vitamins and nutrients.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Even More Lore: In Shadows of Greg, this was made with just Distilled Water and Mince Meat. How far we've come...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "The final processing line, at last!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Sterilized Growth Medium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "The ZPM Lapotronic Orb is even better than the LuV one! It of course comes at a price...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: Imagine making 64 IV or 8 LuV Orbs for just one of these! See the Lore for the LuV orb for details.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "A power singularity!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Mega Batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "This Quest will be completed with either a ZPM Energy Hatch... or a ZPM Dynamo Hatch for your Multiblock Generators.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/zpm__zero_point_module.snbt",
          "text": "Don't forget that 16 Amp variants exist!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "zpm__zero_point_module",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "UV voltage-tier components and machine parts."
    },

      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:crushed_diamond_ore",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Drop in Water to get Purified Ore",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Crushed Diamond Ore",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:impure_diamond_dust",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Drop in Water to get Clean Dust",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Impure Pile of Diamond Dust",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven is an electric equivalent to the Coke Oven. It can produce Coal Coke and Charcoal with a Creosote byproduct just as before, but also has additional Organic Byproducts, the most beneficial of these being Wood Tar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Another product is Biomass, which is more pertinent for your progression as of this moment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The byproducts can be Distilled for an array of organic resources, with different byproducts giving different ratios of different chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need to take a peek at the Distillation Quest to get to grips with the power of distillation...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are recipes that can accept Nitrogen to double the speed. This isn't quite worth pursuing right now, but Nitrogen will eventually be free and infinite.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You should be more than fine with a single LV Energy Hatch, and Superconductors to supply a steady 64 EU/t. Additionally, Cupronickel Coils are perfect when you're getting used to the Pyrolyse Oven, but you may want to upgrade to Kanthal eventually.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Plant path is one path you can take towards Ethylene, which is a necessary chemical in progressing to HV. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven remains optional. However, it is very rewarding to construct one either for Power, or for the various Byproducts, and we highly recommend you give it a look.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: In GT5u, the structure for the Pyrolyse Oven was infamously a giant box with only 9 coils inside. Players usually spent several minutes debugging what is wrong due to a lack of a EMI preview.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Thermal Decomposition",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Pyrolyse Oven",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to the organic Ethylene path! The goal here is to produce Ethanol and mix it with Sulfuric Acid to make Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To get Ethanol, you'll need to distill it from Biomass—so let's look at the different ways to produce that biomass.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The first method is simple: put saplings into a Brewery with water. The downside? It's slow.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The second method is more complex, but yields much better results. You’ll need to get Bio Chaff, which comes from macerating Plant Balls. Note that you won't get any extra chance until you unlock HV Crushing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plant Balls can be obtained by:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging Sugarcane or Beets",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging every wood that you can tap #afc:tappable_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Compressing saplings directly",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, process your Bio Chaff with water in a Brewery or go for a Pyrolyse Oven for a 66 percent boost in output.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You may recognise Plantballs from IndustrialCraft2! In IC2 Experimental, there was this rather... obscure way to turn Biomass into Biogas for power, which sadly required too much investment to be really worth it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plants aren't industrial enough!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery can be used to make Lubricant from Redstone and Creosote/Oil. Lubricant has some niche uses, namely being used in the Cutter to significantly reduce the duration of its recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery is a very, very slow machine, but it requires almost no power to run its recipes. Building many Breweries will help you obtain enough Biomass on your quest for plastic.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Brewery unlocks the Biomass path towards Ethylene. The same can be done in the Pyrolyse Oven. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You could make your own Brewery if you want to build up a company and start selling Booze... in GregTech 6.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "*hic*... *hic*",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_brewery)item(gtceu:mv_brewery))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Local Brewery selling Booze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Our warmest congratulations on making it here.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The way you should obtain Ethylene was described in the previous Quest, depending on the route you chose.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ethylene will shortly be used in polymer production. It's also useful in a few other reactions.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "One of many hydrocarbons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Put your previously made Biomass into a MV Distillery to make Ethanol.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this for Ethylene by reacting it with Sulfuric Acid in a MV Chemical Reactor... unless you chose to go down the Oil path.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you have a hefty starting stock of Ethanol, consider making and distilling Fermented Biomass in a Distillation Tower (when you get there!) for extra chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Do not try to use Ethanol as a Power source... it's quite possibly its worst use. If it's organic Power that you want, we suggest taking a look at Benzene instead!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Yo, wait - that's alcohol!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Distillating Oil or Raw Oil will give you Fuel that you will need to desulfurize.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hydrogen Sulfide is perfectly recycled in an Electrolyzer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To automate this process, simply place your Chemical Reactor and your Electrolyzer next to each other. Be sure to use your Screwdriver to enable input from the output side.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha is a good Product source, surprsingly it's also a really good fuel (we heard nerf is coming) but if you are sitting on Oil sand or you don't have Raw Oil then Light Oil may be better. Keep following the quests to the right for more details.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We're still confused on how Naphtha is pronounced",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha or Light Oil Residue",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't panic! We'll mostly be doing this to get hydrocarbons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are many ways to acquire Ethylene from Oil processing. It's easy to get lost in all the options and recipes!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The best way is to steam-crack Naphtha in a Chemical Reactor. Be sure to make the Severely Steam-Cracked version for a higher yield of Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once in HV, you'll have the option to do this recipe in the Cracking Unit at 100 percent efficiency. Unfortunately, the recipes you are doing with the Chemical Reactor have a loss of 50 percent.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: All of this looks and feels terribly inefficient - we hope that's strong motivation to tier up! The Distillation Tower will be a huge upgrade for petrochem, but you're not quite there... yet. Right now, you'll have to use a Distillery to get Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fuel and oil cracking can get complicated...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fuel Cracking",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hello there, and welcome to MV Energy Production.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "In this quest, we’ll go over the different options available to keep your MV machines powered and running smoothly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We won’t cover this in every chapter, so we highly recommend that you read the dedicated chapter on GregTech Energy for a deeper understanding of how everything works.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For now, here are your three main options:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use an MV Generator to directly produce MV-level energy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Step up your LV power by using transformers, with a 4:1 ratio between LV and MV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Convert RF using your Alternator to get MV energy directly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "tips__tools",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "The only thing separating man from animal is our use of Tools. Except for apes, monkeys, dolphins, crows, otters, octo....... Use your skills and tools to make the most of this world, and save yourself some effort.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "It's all a hammer anyways.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "This is your first Sophisticated Backpack. It has a total of 36 inventory slots and can hold one upgrade. Check the two branches after this to learn about inventory and backpack upgrades.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "No, the map won't magically appear.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Your First Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "A Red Steel Backpack is your first proper backpack inventory upgrade. It has a total slot count of 54 and can hold two upgrades.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Cooler than a Blue Steel Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Red Steel Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Backpack Upgrades can be installed in your Sophisticated Backpacks, giving your backpack new abilities and utilities.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need access to Steel to be able to craft these upgrade bases.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "More than just a backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Backpack Upgrades",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "An Aluminium Backpack is your second proper backpack inventory upgrade. It has a total slot count of 81 and can hold three upgrades.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Is it Aluminum or Aluminium?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Aluminium Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "A Titanium Backpack is your third proper backpack inventory upgrade. It has a total slot count of 108 and can hold five upgrades.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Almost Titanic.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "The Crafting Upgrade lets you carry a Workbench in your backpack wherever you go. You'll wonder how you lived without it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "A Workbench on the go!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Crafting Upgrade",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Certain backpack upgrades have Advanced Upgrade versions. They have more functionality, such as allowing extra filter types or being more configurable.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Try crafting a Filter and Advanced Filter upgrade to complete this quest.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Upgrades but yellow",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Advanced Upgrades",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "These are all the backpack upgrades Sophisticated Backpacks has to offer. Can you craft them all?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "You can't even fit all of these in a single backpack...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "ALL THE UPGRADES",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "With MEGA Cells, you can create cells with Ludicrous data storage!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Fun Fact: A single 256M Portable Item Cell can store up to one billion of a single item type! That's enough to fill over 58 thousand chests!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "When Kilobytes aren't enough",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "MEGA Portable Item and Fluid Cells",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "A Toolbelt can hold 7 extra Pouches, that's 9 tools in one belt!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "That belt must be very heavy at this point...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "nbt(fuzzy:{Size:9.0d})",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain a maxed out toolbelt.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "ALL THE TOOLS",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "With a Toolbelt, you'll be able to manage multiple tools without wasting inventory slots! This handy item can go into a special Toolbelt Slot in your Curios menu. You can insert and retrieve tools with a keybind. It starts with only two slots, but more can be added by adding Belt Pouches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Those tools sure take up a lot of space, let's fix that!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Toolbelt and Pouches",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "With Applied Energistics 2, you can create Portable Item and Fluid Cells, which allow you to store large amounts of items and fluids as pure, raw data. Sadly, Applied Energistics 2 is locked behind HV, so it might be a while until you can create these.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Isn't this too advanced?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Portable Item and Fluid Cells",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "There are two options for backpacks: the Frame Pack or the Sophisticated Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "A Frame Pack is a dense backpack allowing you to store up to 64 items in each slot no matter their standard stack size.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "A Sophisticated Backpack is the primary way to expand your portable inventory size going forward. The first tier will more than double your normal inventory, and further tiers increase it even more! It also comes with a wide variety of functional upgrades, and different parts of it can be dyed in different colors!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Sleeping bag not included",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Materials for a Backpack",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Small Vessels have 4 inventory slots while Large Vessels have 9! The Small Vessel is not just for item storage, but is also necessary for smelting and alloying basic metals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "The Large Vessel is heavier to carry, but is great for preserving food.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Large Vessels double as early backpacks!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Clay can be quite useful",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {

          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:fired_vessels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "Large and Small Vessels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__tools",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__tools.snbt",
          "text": "In your travels, you may explore a whole lot and end up picking up a lot of junk. This quest branch provides several options for expanding your on-the-go inventory.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__tools",
          "item_ref_count": 32
