      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Sylvite replaces basalt at any level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The Moon",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The Moon",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "Congratulations on reaching the moon! The moon is a barren wasteland, grey and dead, as far as the eye can see; however, underground is where you will find the next major resource in Ad Astra - Desh! Desh is used to craft advanced machines, space stations, and the tier two rocket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The environment",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The environment of the moon is very different from earth. As the moon has no oxygen and is freezing, attempting to place water will freeze it and lit blocks like torches will break. Any plant life and crops will also die very quickly. To combat these effects, you can build an oxygen distributor, which will we cover later.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "Returning Home",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "To return back to earth, open your lander (shift + right-click) and collect the rocket, fuel and the launch pad. To remove the lander, punch it. Set up the launch pad, place the rocket down and fill it up with fuel. Press and fly back to earth.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Super Batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Autoclave - despite being optional - is a machine you'll definitively want to grab.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This unlocks unique Batteries that are far superior to their standard counterparts.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Home-grown batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Autoclave",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Chemical Bath is used to dye Lenses using Chemical Dye. This will let you engrave more types of Wafers, and cool other kinds of Hot Ingots.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Please don't bathe with your toaster...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Bathing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You now have access to two new Maintenance Hatches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Automatic Maintenance Hatch is pretty simple - it eliminates the need for Maintenance, forever.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Configurable Maintenance Hatch is more interesting. You can configure it to cut off 10%% duration on recipes, at the cost of making Maintenance happen three times as fast. That is 16 real hours of activity. Additionally, you can use it on Multiblock Generators to increase the duration of the recipe, which indirectly increases their fuel efficiency by 10%%!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Reminder: You can put Tape in the maintenance Hatch to automatically fix problems.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The Automatic Maintenance Hatch texture and mechanic comes from TecTech, an addon mod originally made for GregTech: New Horizons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You'll see more from TecTech if you stick with us with the late game.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I have become maintenance, eater of tape",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Just like MV, you'll need to make higher-tier components.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The painful Flawless Gems have been replaced with slightly-more-painful Vitrified Ender Pearls. Also notice that the Motors require 2x Silver Cables.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hulls and Machines use Gold Cables.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, a use for that stockpile of Gold!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Tips to make life less painful:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use Electrotine decomposition and Ore Processing for Electrum.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use the Chemical Bath with Mercury for Gold and Silver from a wide variety of ores.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We call this \"fun\"",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Assembler is the next step in progression, but also unlocks a handful of useful utilities - check around this quest for more.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this HV Assembler to make your Rocket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Assembler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You'll want to grab these two lenses. The Simple System on Chip will be used for the best ULV Circuit recipe, while the LPIC Wafer is required for your next Energy Hatch.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I see the world in colour",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "ULV Circuits are used in some AE2 recipes and Create recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Best ULV circuits!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Low Power Integrated Circuit requires an HV Cutter inside your Cleanroom.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Energy Hatches will be nice additions so you can run your Multiblocks (mainly your Electric Blast Furnace and Large Chemical Reactor) directly off a HV line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Noticing a pattern here?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Upgrade your EBF II",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Chemical Reactor unlocks a ton of new recipes. Ah, who doesn't love the ever-growing demand on more advanced Chemistry?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: We've actually unlocked a ton of new content by reaching HV. However, not every processing line or item is crucial right now. It'd make the HV chapter far too dense if we included everything!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You could still check out the EV Chapter if you want to push yourself.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Chemistry",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium requires a slightly more involved process than the previous materials to process. To get started, you'll need Rutile, which can be obtained by processing Ilmenite that you can get from Bauxite, Aluminium, Armalcolite and Desh, which can be found in massive quantities on The Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you've gotten enough Rutile, your journey to Titanium continues in the next quest. There's no need to rush things.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],

        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/leather",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/leather.json",
          "text": "Genuine Leather",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Genuine Leather",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/leather",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/leather.json",
          "text": "Finish preparing the hide by soaking it in water then tannin",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Genuine Leather",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "Leather Making",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "Leather is a sturdy material formed from animal hides. It is required to craft Armor, Saddles, or a Bellows. Raw Hides must be treated through several processes: soaking, scraping, preparing, and tanning to turn them into Leather.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "Raw Hide",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "To get started you will first need to obtain Raw Hide, which is dropped when slaughtering many different Animals. Different animals will drop different sizes of Raw Hide.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "Leather making will require several materials and tools. You will need: - Limewater - a solution of Flux in Water - Water - Tannin - an acidic solution made from the bark of some trees. - Raw Hides With all that on hand, you are ready to begin the leather working process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "First, raw hides must be soaked to clean them and loosen unwanted material before processing. This can be done by sealing some Raw Hide in a Barrel of Limewater for at least eight hours.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "After soaking, Soaked Hides must be scraped which removes any excess material. In order to scrape hides, place the hide on the side of a Log. With a Knife, on each part of the hide and it will start to change texture.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "Once the hide is fully scraped, it can be broken to pick up a Scraped Hide.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "After scraping, Scraped Hides must be sealed in a Barrel of Water for at least eight hours, for one final cleaning before tanning.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/leather_making.json",
          "text": "Finally, Prepared Hides must be sealed in a Barrel of Tannin, which is an acidic chemical compound that helps to convert the hide to Leather. After another eight hours, you can remove the Leather from the barrel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Leather Making",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fireproof",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Fireproof",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Craft some fire clay",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Fireproof",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fire Clay",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "The list of uses of fire clay is small, but all of them are important. Fire clay is a stronger variant of clay that has better heat resistance. It is used to make things that have to get very hot!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fire clay is made from graphite powder, crushed in a quern, as well as kaolinite powder",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Kaolinite powder is made by heating Kaolin Clay. However, the process is not perfect, and only 20% of clay will form powder!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "The Crucible in its unfired state is made from fire clay.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace only accepts fire bricks as insulation.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fire Ingot Molds are a stronger type of Ingot Mold that has just a 1 in 100 chance of breaking, compared to 1 in 10 for a regular ingot mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fire Clay",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "The list of uses of fire clay is small, but all of them are important. Fire clay is a stronger variant of clay that has better heat resistance. It is used to make things that have to get very hot!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fire clay is made from graphite powder, crushed in a quern, as well as kaolinite powder",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Kaolinite powder is made by heating Kaolin Clay. However, the process is not perfect, and only 20%% of clay will form powder!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "The Crucible in its unfired state is made from fire clay.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace only accepts fire bricks as insulation.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/fire_clay.json",
          "text": "Fire Ingot Molds are a stronger type of Ingot Mold that has just a 1 in 100 chance of breaking, compared to 1 in 10 for a regular ingot mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Fire Clay",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops are a source of food and some other materials. While each crop is slightly different, crops all have some similar principles. In order to start growing crops, you will need some Seeds, which can be obtained by searching for Wild Crops and breaking them. Once you have obtained seeds, you will also need a Hoe. Seeds are also useful as bait.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "In addition to finding wild crops, seeds can also be produced from existing crops. When a fully mature crop dies - either by weather, or leaving it to rot - it will go to seed, dropping more seeds that can be used to grow even more crops next season.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "- Barley - Beet - Cabbage - Canola - Carrot - Cucumber - Flax - Garlic - Green Bean - Jute - Lentil - Maize - Melon",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "- Oat - Onion - Papyrus - Potato - Pumpkin - Radish - Red Bell Pepper - Rice - Rye - Soybean - Squash - Sugarcane - Sunflower - Tomato - Wheat - Yellow Bell Pepper",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "To get started, a Stone Hoe can be knapped as seen above.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Once the hoe head is knapped, it can be crafted into a Hoe. Hoes function as in Vanilla, by right clicking dirt blocks to turn them into Farmland. They can also be used to convert Rooted Dirt into Dirt.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "All crops need to be planted on farmland in order to grow. Some crops have additional requirements such as being waterlogged or requiring a stick to grow on. Crops do not need nutrients to grow, but they certainly help. There are three nutrients: Nitrogen, Phosphorous, and Potassium. Each crop has a favorite nutrient.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Growing Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Consuming its favorite nutrient causes a crop to grow faster, and improves the yield of the crop at harvest time. That means that crops that consumed more nutrients drop more food when broken! Consuming a nutrient also has the effect of replenishing the other nutrients around it a small amount. The next several pages list all the crops present in TFC",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -8 - 26 °C Hydration: 18 - 75 % Nutrient: Nitrogen Barley is a single block crop. Barley seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Barley and Barley Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 3 - 40 °C Hydration: 35 - 100 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Oat is a single block crop. Oat seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Oat and Oat Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -11 - 30 °C Hydration: 25 - 85 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Rye is a single block crop. Rye seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Rye and Rye Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 13 - 40 °C Hydration: 75 - 100 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Maize is a two block tall crop. Maize seeds can be planted on farmland, will grow two blocks tall, and will produce Maize and Maize Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -4 - 35 °C Hydration: 25 - 100 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Wheat is a single block crop. Wheat seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Wheat and Wheat Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 15 - 30 °C Hydration: 25 - 100 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Rice is a single block crop. Rice must be grown underwater - it must be planted on farmland, in freshwater that is a single block deep. It will produce Rice and Rice Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Note: in order to grow, the rice block must be Waterlogged.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -5 - 20 °C Hydration: 18 - 85 % Nutrient: Potassium Beets are a a single block crop. Beet seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Beet and Beet Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -10 - 27 °C Hydration: 15 - 65 % Nutrient: Nitrogen Cabbage is a single block crop. Cabbage seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Cabbage and Cabbage Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 3 - 30 °C Hydration: 25 - 100 % Nutrient: Potassium Carrot is a single block crop. Carrot seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Carrot and Carrot Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -20 - 18 °C Hydration: 15 - 75 % Nutrient: Nitrogen Garlic is a single block crop. Garlic seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Garlic and Garlic Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 2 - 35 °C Hydration: 38 - 100 % Nutrient: Nitrogen Green Beans is a climbing two block tall crop. Green Bean seeds can be planted on farmland, will grow two blocks tall if a stick is present, and will produce Green Beans and Green Bean Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Green Beans",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.title",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "The stick is required in order for the crop to fully grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: -1 - 37 °C Hydration: 50 - 100 % Nutrient: Potassium Potatoes are a single block crop. Potato seeds can be planted on farmland and will produce Potatoes and Potato Seeds as a product.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 0 - 30 °C Hydration: 30 - 80 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Pumpkins are a spreading crop. Pumpkin seeds can be planted on farmland and will place up to two Pumpkin Blocks on the ground next to it while it is mature. If the pumpkin blocks are harvested, and the plant matures again, it can grow more pumpkins. Pumpkins can be made into Jack 'o Lanterns.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.32.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 5 - 37 °C Hydration: 75 - 100 % Nutrient: Phosphorus Melons are a spreading crop. Melon seeds can be planted on farmland and will place up to two Melon Blocks on the ground next to it while it is mature. If the melon blocks are harvested, and the plant matures again, it can grow more melon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.34.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 16 - 30 °C Hydration: 25 - 60 % Nutrient: Potassium Red Bell Peppers are a pickable crop. When they are near mature, they can be harvested with to obtain Green Bell Peppers. Harvesting with allows the plant to grow more peppers in the future rather than destroying the plant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.36.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Red Bell Peppers",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.36.title",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 16 - 30 °C Hydration: 25 - 60 % Nutrient: Potassium Yellow Bell Peppers are a pickable crop. When they are near mature, they can be harvested with to obtain Green Bell Peppers. Harvesting with allows the plant to grow more peppers in the future rather than destroying the plant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.38.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 66
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crops.json",
          "text": "Yellow Bell Peppers",

      "id": "pack:tfg/unfired_pottery",
      "label": "Unfired Pottery",

      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft Blast Furnace Tips",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "So, you finally found that Kaolinite and are probably feeling pretty excited about making a 5-level Blast Furnace. Here are some tips before you go wild!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "Before you use all your fire clay on 20 fire bricks, note that once you get into the steam age, you can craft it for significantly cheaper. This allows you to save Kaolinite and Graphite for Casting Tables.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "Preheating Metal",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "To convert the iron to steel, the blast furnace must heat the iron inside to Brilliant White. On the first operation, the metal warms up along with the blast furnace. However, subsequent operations will take a similar amount of time despite the furnace being at temp already, as it needs to heat the metal from cold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "If the metal is hot when added into the blast furnace, it will take less time to reach brilliant white. Consider heating your metal in a Charcoal Forge before putting it into an already-hot furnace to save time, especially with smaller blast furnaces that process less at a time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "You'll need lots of Steel for the Steam and LV ages. Lots of steel. The Electric Blast Furnace is far off, so maybe think about optimizing your steel production.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "You can pump liquid directly out of the blast furnace using a Create Mechanical Pump. Pump into a Mold Table and extract the ingots with a hopper or chute: Easy automation! You can automate the bellows with a Deployer too! Just be careful, as using the bellows excessively consumes more fuel and breaks your tuyere faster. You can process the resulting Pig Iron and High Carbon Steel in a Forge Hammer or Helve Hammer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "You can automatically replace broken tuyeres with a hopper or item pipe facing into the blast furnace.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },
