      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "lv__low_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Fluid Regulator is sort of the equivalent to a Robot Arm for Fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "It can transfer fluids, but has two useful modes:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Supply Exact will transfer the amount of specified Fluid per tick if available. No more, no less.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Keep Exact will make sure the exact amount of Fluid in the attached machine is being kept.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You won't need it much, because natively GT machines will fill only one slot with the Pump Cover",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "A cover that you may not use much",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LV Fluid Regulator",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "When placed on a machine, Robot Arms are a more configurable version of a Conveyor Module. They allow you to transfer items in specific batches, at specific rates, or keep a certain amount of items stocked.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Practical uses for the Robot Arm will be explained in future Quests as a tutorial.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The functionalities provided by the Robot Arm work only if it is required to perform the operation.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: GregTech 5 had blocks such as the Buffer, Filter and Type Filter to allow fine control over items. In GTCE it's all been replaced by the almighty Robot Arm and various Filter Covers. Much more compact!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You're going to hate making these",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LV Robot Arm",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Less common as a component, but used in a number of critical machines.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "When placed on a machine, conveyor modules will transfer items. You can configure them to import or export.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "They can also accept various Item Filters.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "A crafting component, but also a cover!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LV Conveyor Module",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "When placed on a machine, pumps will transfer fluid. You can configure them to import or export.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "It can accept a Fluid Filter.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LV Electric Pump",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "A specialized motor needed to make certain machines.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "One of the more intricate crafting components, as it's made using a motor.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "A crafting component - did you expect anything fancier?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "LV Electric Piston",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Steam Turbine is most players' first LV generator, as you've already got steam production set up from your Steam machines.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "When provided Steam, the turbine will output EU at a ratio of 2 mB = 1 EU.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "One downside of steam is transporting it, and due to its lack of energy density, you'll need large or huge pipes to carry it around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You may also consider upgrading to the Large Bronze Boiler once you start needing more power than what your single-block boilers can produce.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You must construct additional turbines",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Steam Turbine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This questline serves as a quick guide to GregTech Amperage. As for Voltage mechanics, we'll go over those once you reach MV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "TL;DR: For safety, match the Amperage limit of your Cables with the max amount of Amps provided by your Energy Producers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Still confused? Check the GregTech Energy chapter for a full rundown on GregTech's electricity system!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Amps and You: a how-to",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "But what are Amps?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Well... can you show us an MV Circuit as well?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Aluminium is the main ingredient for MV machinery. Get ready to need a lot of it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The process of smelting Aluminium can be cut down by about a third with Nitrogen, which is obtained by centrifuging Air from a Gas Collector.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will still need MV Circuits in order to make MV machines!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We're basically in MV now, right?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Your first Aluminium Ingot",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Make some Battery Alloy, used for Battery Hulls.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Antimony is found in Stibnite, look through your TFC Field Guide for more information on where to find it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: This quest does not contain functional Batteries - head downwards for that.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Batteries are great!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Battery Preparations!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Covers are placed by right-clicking with Cover in hand on a face of a machine, and removed with a Crowbar (or by mining the machine, but that's way slower).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Pretty much any GregTech Tile-Entity will accept Covers, even if they wouldn't provide any functionality.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Opening a Cover is done by sneak-right-clicking it with an empty hand. You can also right-click with a Screwdriver, or sneak-right-click on another face using the grid.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For Covers that transfer Items/Fluids, you will have a button to enable/disable other forms of transfer through the cover:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Disabled: only the cover will be allowed to transfer on this machine side.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Allow Filtered (only relevant with a filter!): other transfer will be enabled only if it matches the filter",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Allow Unfiltered: any other transfer is allowed on this machine side.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Placing a cover of a lower voltage tier on a machine is safe.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Extra utility without taking up blockspace!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I'm an expert on Covers now, don't you worry.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Cover Behaviour",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "All machines typically have one Front Face, and one Output Face.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Front Face should be obvious.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/lv__low_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/lv__low_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Output Face is the face with a dot or hole on it. By default, this is at the back of the machine as you place it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "lv__low_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "LV voltage-tier components and machine parts."
    },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "hv__high_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Energium Crystals store a significant amount of EU. They're also arguably cheaper.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Science-fiction batteries!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Super Batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Autoclave - despite being optional - is a machine you'll definitively want to grab.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "This unlocks unique Batteries that are far superior to their standard counterparts.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Home-grown batteries",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Autoclave",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Chemical Bath is used to dye Lenses using Chemical Dye. This will let you engrave more types of Wafers, and cool other kinds of Hot Ingots.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Please don't bathe with your toaster...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Bathing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You now have access to two new Maintenance Hatches.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Automatic Maintenance Hatch is pretty simple - it eliminates the need for Maintenance, forever.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Configurable Maintenance Hatch is more interesting. You can configure it to cut off 10%% duration on recipes, at the cost of making Maintenance happen three times as fast. That is 16 real hours of activity. Additionally, you can use it on Multiblock Generators to increase the duration of the recipe, which indirectly increases their fuel efficiency by 10%%!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Reminder: You can put Tape in the maintenance Hatch to automatically fix problems.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The Automatic Maintenance Hatch texture and mechanic comes from TecTech, an addon mod originally made for GregTech: New Horizons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You'll see more from TecTech if you stick with us with the late game.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I have become maintenance, eater of tape",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Advanced Maintenance",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Just like MV, you'll need to make higher-tier components.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The painful Flawless Gems have been replaced with slightly-more-painful Vitrified Ender Pearls. Also notice that the Motors require 2x Silver Cables.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hulls and Machines use Gold Cables.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, a use for that stockpile of Gold!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Tips to make life less painful:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use Electrotine decomposition and Ore Processing for Electrum.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use the Chemical Bath with Mercury for Gold and Silver from a wide variety of ores.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We call this \"fun\"",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Assembler is the next step in progression, but also unlocks a handful of useful utilities - check around this quest for more.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this HV Assembler to make your Rocket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Assembler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You'll want to grab these two lenses. The Simple System on Chip will be used for the best ULV Circuit recipe, while the LPIC Wafer is required for your next Energy Hatch.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "I see the world in colour",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "ULV Circuits are used in some AE2 recipes and Create recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Best ULV circuits!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Low Power Integrated Circuit requires an HV Cutter inside your Cleanroom.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Energy Hatches will be nice additions so you can run your Multiblocks (mainly your Electric Blast Furnace and Large Chemical Reactor) directly off a HV line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Noticing a pattern here?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Upgrade your EBF II",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The HV Chemical Reactor unlocks a ton of new recipes. Ah, who doesn't love the ever-growing demand on more advanced Chemistry?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: We've actually unlocked a ton of new content by reaching HV. However, not every processing line or item is crucial right now. It'd make the HV chapter far too dense if we included everything!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You could still check out the EV Chapter if you want to push yourself.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "High Voltage Chemistry",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium requires a slightly more involved process than the previous materials to process. To get started, you'll need Rutile, which can be obtained by processing Ilmenite that you can get from Bauxite, Aluminium, Armalcolite and Desh, which can be found in massive quantities on The Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you've gotten enough Rutile, your journey to Titanium continues in the next quest. There's no need to rush things.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Ore processing for Bauxite is ridiculously good! It can easily triples your Rutile yield.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "...means it WASN'T Titanium!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Almost Titanium",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To get your grubby hands on some Titanium, Rutile must first be reduced to Titanium Tetrachloride in an HV Chemical Reactor, then reduced again in an EBF with Magnesium to finally arrive at Titanium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Carbon, Chlorine, Oxygen, and Magnesium used in the process can be perfectly recovered from the byproduct Carbon Monoxide and Magnesium Chloride with no loss. If you set up a system to recover them, the only material that you'll need to provide for Titanium is Rutile!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Titanium Tetrachloride also acts as a catalyst in the production of plastics. A touch of TiCl₄ in the polymerisation process can increase the polymer yield to 133%%. Naturally, this is completely optional.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Is it titanium yet?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Almost Titanium²",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "By this point, normal Cables have such insignificant loss that superconductors aren't a requirement. That being said, they're still useful for being able to split and combine cables on the fly, and these ones are half oxygen so they're cheap too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Superconductors are still required to craft Field Generators.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "HV Superconductors",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Nichrome Heating Coils increase the temperature of your Blast Furnace to 3,600K.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/hv__high_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/hv__high_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "These will be required to upgrade your primary EBF so you can make Titanium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "hv__high_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven is an electric equivalent to the Coke Oven. It can produce Coal Coke and Charcoal with a Creosote byproduct just as before, but also has additional Organic Byproducts, the most beneficial of these being Wood Tar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Another product is Biomass, which is more pertinent for your progression as of this moment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The byproducts can be Distilled for an array of organic resources, with different byproducts giving different ratios of different chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need to take a peek at the Distillation Quest to get to grips with the power of distillation...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are recipes that can accept Nitrogen to double the speed. This isn't quite worth pursuing right now, but Nitrogen will eventually be free and infinite.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You should be more than fine with a single LV Energy Hatch, and Superconductors to supply a steady 64 EU/t. Additionally, Cupronickel Coils are perfect when you're getting used to the Pyrolyse Oven, but you may want to upgrade to Kanthal eventually.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Plant path is one path you can take towards Ethylene, which is a necessary chemical in progressing to HV. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven remains optional. However, it is very rewarding to construct one either for Power, or for the various Byproducts, and we highly recommend you give it a look.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: In GT5u, the structure for the Pyrolyse Oven was infamously a giant box with only 9 coils inside. Players usually spent several minutes debugging what is wrong due to a lack of a EMI preview.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Thermal Decomposition",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Pyrolyse Oven",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to the organic Ethylene path! The goal here is to produce Ethanol and mix it with Sulfuric Acid to make Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To get Ethanol, you'll need to distill it from Biomass—so let's look at the different ways to produce that biomass.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The first method is simple: put saplings into a Brewery with water. The downside? It's slow.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The second method is more complex, but yields much better results. You’ll need to get Bio Chaff, which comes from macerating Plant Balls. Note that you won't get any extra chance until you unlock HV Crushing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plant Balls can be obtained by:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging Sugarcane or Beets",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging every wood that you can tap #afc:tappable_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Compressing saplings directly",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, process your Bio Chaff with water in a Brewery or go for a Pyrolyse Oven for a 66 percent boost in output.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You may recognise Plantballs from IndustrialCraft2! In IC2 Experimental, there was this rather... obscure way to turn Biomass into Biogas for power, which sadly required too much investment to be really worth it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plants aren't industrial enough!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery can be used to make Lubricant from Redstone and Creosote/Oil. Lubricant has some niche uses, namely being used in the Cutter to significantly reduce the duration of its recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery is a very, very slow machine, but it requires almost no power to run its recipes. Building many Breweries will help you obtain enough Biomass on your quest for plastic.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Brewery unlocks the Biomass path towards Ethylene. The same can be done in the Pyrolyse Oven. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You could make your own Brewery if you want to build up a company and start selling Booze... in GregTech 6.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "*hic*... *hic*",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_brewery)item(gtceu:mv_brewery))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Local Brewery selling Booze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Our warmest congratulations on making it here.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The way you should obtain Ethylene was described in the previous Quest, depending on the route you chose.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ethylene will shortly be used in polymer production. It's also useful in a few other reactions.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "One of many hydrocarbons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Put your previously made Biomass into a MV Distillery to make Ethanol.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this for Ethylene by reacting it with Sulfuric Acid in a MV Chemical Reactor... unless you chose to go down the Oil path.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you have a hefty starting stock of Ethanol, consider making and distilling Fermented Biomass in a Distillation Tower (when you get there!) for extra chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Do not try to use Ethanol as a Power source... it's quite possibly its worst use. If it's organic Power that you want, we suggest taking a look at Benzene instead!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Yo, wait - that's alcohol!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Distillating Oil or Raw Oil will give you Fuel that you will need to desulfurize.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hydrogen Sulfide is perfectly recycled in an Electrolyzer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To automate this process, simply place your Chemical Reactor and your Electrolyzer next to each other. Be sure to use your Screwdriver to enable input from the output side.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha is a good Product source, surprsingly it's also a really good fuel (we heard nerf is coming) but if you are sitting on Oil sand or you don't have Raw Oil then Light Oil may be better. Keep following the quests to the right for more details.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We're still confused on how Naphtha is pronounced",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha or Light Oil Residue",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't panic! We'll mostly be doing this to get hydrocarbons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are many ways to acquire Ethylene from Oil processing. It's easy to get lost in all the options and recipes!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The best way is to steam-crack Naphtha in a Chemical Reactor. Be sure to make the Severely Steam-Cracked version for a higher yield of Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls are a versatile tool which can be used to make Salads, to make Soups, to Salt meat, or to apply Powder to Glass in order to change the resulting glass's color. Bowls can be made both from Ceramic, by knapping a bowl out of clay, and then firing them.",

          "text": "Bowls can also be made out of wood via crafting them together with Glue Glue can be made by soaking Bone Meal in a barrel of Limewater.",

          "text": "Bowls can also be placed on the ground like blocks. When placed, they can be filled with any type of Powder - the results of grinding ores, minerals, or gems in the Quern.",

          "text": "The Powder Bowl can hold up to 16 of a given powder. To insert items, while holding the powder. To extract items, with an empty hand. allows extracting the entire contents of the bowl.",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",

          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon.           "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",

          "label": "ore_processing",

          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts.
