          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

        },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",

        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While there's easier ways to obtain Platinum and Palladium, this line will be mandatory for accessing the others.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ore_processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon. This diagram may look overwhelming at first, but we'll take it step by step.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To Process, Ore Not To Process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I agree to not just dump everything in a furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium persulfate can be a little annoying to mass produce, though if you want, Sea Water is a great starting point.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples that might be worth the investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Not just for circuit board etching",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Sodium Persulfate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Here's some recommendations for Mercury bathing:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

        {

        {

        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "text": "A useful time saver",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium Persulfate bathing examples",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "We say line, but really it's just a two step process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "only_one(item(gtceu:indium_concentrate_bucket)nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{Amount:1000,FluidName:\"gtceu:indium_concentrate\"}}))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While there's easier ways to obtain Platinum and Palladium, this line will be mandatory for accessing the others.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platline can be started at HV, but you won’t be able to complete it fully until later in EV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech players vs Mekanism players when they need Osmium:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfg:platinum_ore_group)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Any #tfg:platinum_ore_group",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Infamous Platline",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "For all your other Purified Ores that doesn't need special treatment, it's best to run them through the Thermal Centrifuge. This machine can produce byproducts at a reliable rate, without needing any other ingredients. It's quite slow, however, so we suggest either making multiple machines or the steam parallel multiblock.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The go-to for most purified ores",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:mv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:hv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:ev_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:iv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:luv_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:zpm_thermal_centrifuge)item(gtceu:uv_thermal_centrifuge)item(tfg:steam_thermal_centrifuge))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Any Thermal Centrifuge",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Thermal Centrifuge",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Sifter should be used for all your Gems. It gives far more yield per ore than any other method, and is the only way to obtain Exquisite and Flawless gems before HV laser engraving.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Martian Boats",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Way off on the far reaches of Mars, you can find both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Crimson",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Warped",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Deep within the nether you can utilize both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats. Format Error: Due to a limitation, Watercrafts created with these materials are NOT impervious to lava, you WILL sink and BURN.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {

          "text": "- Crimson",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",

          "item_ref_count": 8,

          "label": "Good Electronic Circuit",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven is an electric equivalent to the Coke Oven. It can produce Coal Coke and Charcoal with a Creosote byproduct just as before, but also has additional Organic Byproducts, the most beneficial of these being Wood Tar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Another product is Biomass, which is more pertinent for your progression as of this moment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The byproducts can be Distilled for an array of organic resources, with different byproducts giving different ratios of different chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need to take a peek at the Distillation Quest to get to grips with the power of distillation...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are recipes that can accept Nitrogen to double the speed. This isn't quite worth pursuing right now, but Nitrogen will eventually be free and infinite.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You should be more than fine with a single LV Energy Hatch, and Superconductors to supply a steady 64 EU/t. Additionally, Cupronickel Coils are perfect when you're getting used to the Pyrolyse Oven, but you may want to upgrade to Kanthal eventually.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Plant path is one path you can take towards Ethylene, which is a necessary chemical in progressing to HV. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Pyrolyse Oven remains optional. However, it is very rewarding to construct one either for Power, or for the various Byproducts, and we highly recommend you give it a look.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: In GT5u, the structure for the Pyrolyse Oven was infamously a giant box with only 9 coils inside. Players usually spent several minutes debugging what is wrong due to a lack of a EMI preview.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Thermal Decomposition",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Pyrolyse Oven",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to the organic Ethylene path! The goal here is to produce Ethanol and mix it with Sulfuric Acid to make Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To get Ethanol, you'll need to distill it from Biomass—so let's look at the different ways to produce that biomass.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The first method is simple: put saplings into a Brewery with water. The downside? It's slow.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The second method is more complex, but yields much better results. You’ll need to get Bio Chaff, which comes from macerating Plant Balls. Note that you won't get any extra chance until you unlock HV Crushing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plant Balls can be obtained by:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging Sugarcane or Beets",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Centrifuging every wood that you can tap #afc:tappable_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "-Compressing saplings directly",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, process your Bio Chaff with water in a Brewery or go for a Pyrolyse Oven for a 66 percent boost in output.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You may recognise Plantballs from IndustrialCraft2! In IC2 Experimental, there was this rather... obscure way to turn Biomass into Biogas for power, which sadly required too much investment to be really worth it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Plants aren't industrial enough!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery can be used to make Lubricant from Redstone and Creosote/Oil. Lubricant has some niche uses, namely being used in the Cutter to significantly reduce the duration of its recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The Brewery is a very, very slow machine, but it requires almost no power to run its recipes. Building many Breweries will help you obtain enough Biomass on your quest for plastic.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: The Brewery unlocks the Biomass path towards Ethylene. The same can be done in the Pyrolyse Oven. The other path involves Oil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: You could make your own Brewery if you want to build up a company and start selling Booze... in GregTech 6.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "*hic*... *hic*",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:lv_brewery)item(gtceu:mv_brewery))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Local Brewery selling Booze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Our warmest congratulations on making it here.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The way you should obtain Ethylene was described in the previous Quest, depending on the route you chose.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Ethylene will shortly be used in polymer production. It's also useful in a few other reactions.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "One of many hydrocarbons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Put your previously made Biomass into a MV Distillery to make Ethanol.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "You will need this for Ethylene by reacting it with Sulfuric Acid in a MV Chemical Reactor... unless you chose to go down the Oil path.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once you have a hefty starting stock of Ethanol, consider making and distilling Fermented Biomass in a Distillation Tower (when you get there!) for extra chemicals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Do not try to use Ethanol as a Power source... it's quite possibly its worst use. If it's organic Power that you want, we suggest taking a look at Benzene instead!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Yo, wait - that's alcohol!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Distillating Oil or Raw Oil will give you Fuel that you will need to desulfurize.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hydrogen Sulfide is perfectly recycled in an Electrolyzer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "To automate this process, simply place your Chemical Reactor and your Electrolyzer next to each other. Be sure to use your Screwdriver to enable input from the output side.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha is a good Product source, surprsingly it's also a really good fuel (we heard nerf is coming) but if you are sitting on Oil sand or you don't have Raw Oil then Light Oil may be better. Keep following the quests to the right for more details.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We're still confused on how Naphtha is pronounced",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Naphtha or Light Oil Residue",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Don't panic! We'll mostly be doing this to get hydrocarbons.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "There are many ways to acquire Ethylene from Oil processing. It's easy to get lost in all the options and recipes!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "The best way is to steam-crack Naphtha in a Chemical Reactor. Be sure to make the Severely Steam-Cracked version for a higher yield of Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Once in HV, you'll have the option to do this recipe in the Cracking Unit at 100 percent efficiency. Unfortunately, the recipes you are doing with the Chemical Reactor have a loss of 50 percent.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Note: All of this looks and feels terribly inefficient - we hope that's strong motivation to tier up! The Distillation Tower will be a huge upgrade for petrochem, but you're not quite there... yet. Right now, you'll have to use a Distillery to get Ethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fuel and oil cracking can get complicated...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Fuel Cracking",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "Hello there, and welcome to MV Energy Production.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "In this quest, we’ll go over the different options available to keep your MV machines powered and running smoothly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "We won’t cover this in every chapter, so we highly recommend that you read the dedicated chapter on GregTech Energy for a deeper understanding of how everything works.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "For now, here are your three main options:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mv__medium_voltage.snbt",
          "text": "- Use an MV Generator to directly produce MV-level energy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "mv__medium_voltage",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "MV voltage-tier components and machine parts."

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "ore_processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "An important part of GregTech is its Ore Processing Mechanics, but it can get pretty complicated, so this chapter is here to help!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To start with, you should be aware of the Ore Processing Diagram tab in EMI - Press the Show Uses key (default U) on any item related to ore processing and look for the tab with the vanilla Iron Ore icon. This diagram may look overwhelming at first, but we'll take it step by step.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "A major part of GregTech philosophy is that there's not just one way to solve a problem, but we've marked everything important with a star, so if you're completely lost, try following those!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "To Process, Ore Not To Process",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I agree to not just dump everything in a furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While looking at EMI, you've probably noticed that your Macerator or Millstone lists more outputs than what you're actually getting.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "GregTech doesn't communicate this very well, but these extra slots are only available in HV. Once you make it to that tier, the HV macerator becomes incredibly powerful, giving a huge amount of bonus byproducts from each ore.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "I can get even more from my ores!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hv_macerator)item(greate:stainless_steel_crushing_wheel))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Either an HV Macerator or HS Crushing Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Crushing with byproducts",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathing certain ores in Mercury can give some other useful byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Mercury can be easily obtained from Redstone or Cinnabar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples of crushed ores which are good to process in Mercury.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Don't stick your finger in it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Bathe them in Mercury",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Once you get to LV, you may be interested in using a Chemical Bath with Sodium persulfate to get some extra byproducts. Not all ores can be processed this way, but the ones that can, will get around a 70 percent chance of byproducts, compared to the 30 percent of the Ore Washer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Sodium persulfate can be a little annoying to mass produce, though if you want, Sea Water is a great starting point.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The quest below gives some examples that might be worth the investment.",

          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "Not just for circuit board etching",

          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line shouldn't be needed before IV, but it will require purified Sphalerite and Galena.",

        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Indium Line",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platinum Line will be your first real challenge to process, providing you with essential platinum-group metals: Platinum, Palladium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Iridium, and Osmium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "While there's easier ways to obtain Platinum and Palladium, this line will be mandatory for accessing the others.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",
          "text": "The Platline can be started at HV, but you won’t be able to complete it fully until later in EV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "ore_processing",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/ore_processing",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/ore_processing.snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
